- VERIFIED-PLAY-ONLY POLICY — SEARCH-URL PLAY LINKS KILLED ON ARTIST SURFACES (Aug 8, 2026; twin bug report: Discover's Butcher Brown card ▶ "Camp Culture" opened a YouTube search returning a dove-hunting video; Laos gap-fill ▶ buttons returned Mozart-432Hz garbage): TWO ROOT CAUSES, one family — (1) Discover verified the ARTIST on MusicBrainz but shipped the model's knownFor title UNVERIFIED into a quoted search; "Camp Culture" is a FABRICATION (zero hits across MB's 27 Butcher Brown release groups, MB recordings, iTunes' 15 albums, Discogs' 18 releases). (2) The panel ▶ badge was an unconditional search URL; gap-fill artists (absent from MB releases by definition) always degraded to `"name" music`, which YouTube silently loosens into unrelated results for Lao-script names. POLICY (locked; the honest-sparse principle applied to links): a play affordance may ONLY point at a verified artist-specific destination — a playability-checked video, an MB url-rel page (Bandcamp / SoundCloud / artist's YouTube channel / official site), or an Internet Archive item whose creator matches — otherwise NO play button; the pill/read-about link remains. NEW: lib/play/{types,resolve,client}.ts + /api/play/[key] (keys mb:/dg:/wd:/nm:, Netlify Blobs + CDN 30-day cache; chain per artist: queue-cached era video → MB url-rels → IA creator search → null). Panel ▶ renders only AFTER resolution succeeds (client concurrency 3). Discover generation now verifies knownFor against the artist's real MB catalog (unverifiable titles replaced with the artist's actual top release; catalog-less picks dropped) and resolves play/read at generation time; the serving route SANITIZES legacy cached pools (search hrefs stripped → MB read links, knownForVerified:false hides the unverified title) and DiscoverSection normalizes stale CDN payloads too. VERIFIED locally (fixture + live MB): Laos 2004 full panel — ກ. ວຶເສສ, B-Hero and the rest show NO badges, Discogs pills intact; Jamaica 1975 — Marley→his SoundCloud, Lee "Scratch" Perry→leeperry.bandcamp.com, King Tubby→official SoundCloud, all real MB url-rels; Discover — legacy Butcher Brown pick renders "Read about →" (MB), verified picks render real ▶. Wallet-free dev check: `env ANTHROPIC_API_KEY= DISCOVER_FIXTURE=<pool.json> npm run dev` serves a fixture pool through the sanitizer (no model call possible). STILL SEARCH URLS (release-level listen links, separate policy ruling pending): ArtistEraPanel releases, WhatWasPlaying items, GenreStory artist mentions, feed release cards, catalog/album popups, serviceSearchUrl (Spotify/Apple/Amazon), story-card media. PROPOSED, NEEDS GO-AHEAD (~35–45 min local sweep, DISCOGS_TOKEN stays local): Discogs release-videos enrichment for the 493 gap-fill artists → committed verified links (nm:-keyed artists IA-only). DEPLOYED Aug 8 (306d8f9, push via cached credentials): live /api/discover serves the sanitized pool (Butcher Brown card = "Read about →" on earclef.com), live resolver verified. TWO LIVE-VERIFICATION CATCHES, fixed same day: (1) ALEXANDRA INCIDENT — the IA creator match used containment, so the Laos mononym "Alexandra" matched a podcast co-hosted by an "Alexandra Tobor" and earned a garbage badge; match is now EXACT normalized equality and the play Blobs cache key bumped to v2/ so poisoned v1 entries never serve. (2) STORED SEARCH HREFS — the follow queue (Blobs) holds listenHrefs written by the old code; PendingStrip rendered "Balmorhea ▶" as a YouTube search. lib/play/storedHref.ts now guards every stored href at render (PendingStrip: navigation pill, no ▶; Studio queue: "▶ Listen" only for non-reference destinations, else "About"). Durable data outlives code — sanitize at render, always.
- ORIGIN, NOT RESIDENCE — COUNTRY POOLS CORRECTED (Aug 8, 2026; third layer of the 9d97588 rule): ROOT CAUSE CONFIRMED — MusicBrainz's artist `country:` search field derives from the artist's AREA (current residence / citizenship), not begin-area. Tina Turner reads area=Switzerland, begin-area=Nutbush (City), so country:CH claimed her at #1 and country:US did not. NO QUERY-SIDE FIX EXISTS: `beginarea:Switzerland` returns 723 vs 11,501 for country:CH — MB does not index area hierarchies for beginarea, so birthplace must be RESOLVED up the part-of chain (the states pipeline's method). NOT UNIQUE TO HER: scripts/build-origin-corrections.mjs swept 55 countries (big markets + small/wealthy expat magnets) × 200 tag-weight-ranked artists, resolving every begin-area → country: 518 artists are claimed by residence, 34 of them inside a top 10 (worst: IL 42, FR 28, AU 24, AT 23, RU 22, CH 20, AE 18-of-101; clean: MC, LI, ID, LA). CRITICAL FINDING — BIRTHPLACE ALONE IS NOT ORIGIN EITHER: the raw sweep would have stripped Davido from Nigeria, Luis Miguel from Mexico, Alan Walker from Norway, Adamo from Belgium, Alison Hinds from Barbados, Paparizou and Callas from Greece — born abroad, musically wholly of the country. That is the MIRROR of the bug, so scripts/arbitrate-origin-corrections.mjs judges every flagged artist against Wikidata before anything moves, evidence ordered: (1) "<Nationality>-born …" in the curated description — where they STARTED, decisive even after naturalisation (Tina Turner: "American-born Swiss singer", which is why citizenship alone could not fix her); (2) a non-musical profession with no musical word = bad MBID→Wikidata join, never act (caught Gustav Mahler resolving to a German mathematician); (3) citizenship of the pool country → keep; (4) the country's nationality adjective in the description → keep (Nora en Pure, "South African-Swiss DJ", stays Swiss); (5) no Wikidata evidence → keep, no opinion. RESULT: 518 flagged → 56 published, 462 kept. Nine documented KEEP_OVERRIDES where the automated rules are literally right and editorially wrong: Lully and Offenbach (foreign-born founders of French opera/operetta), Robert Miles (Fleurier-born, Italian records), Barry Gibb (Isle of Man-born, Bee Gees began in Brisbane), Vicente Ascone, Serkin, Schmidt, Godowsky (shifting-empire birthplaces), and YASMIN LEVY — MB's area tree resolved Jerusalem to PS; this project does not adjudicate that, so she stays put. DATA: lib/explore/origin-corrections.json holds {from,to,name,begin,end,weight,tags,why} per MBID; lib/explore/originCorrections.ts applies it in fetchOriginArtists for COUNTRY pools only (subdivisions/states are already begin-area based) — a move drops the artist from `from` and adds them to `to` with their weight, tags and life-span so they rank and era-filter normally. VERIFIED: CH 1985 and 2005 have no Tina Turner and lead with Coroner, Yello, Celtic Frost, Eluveitie, Sybreed, The Young Gods, Krokus; she now sits in the US 1975 pool. GAP-FILL AUDITED TOO: its Wikidata query used P27 (citizenship) — same class of bug. All 67 entries carrying origin data were checked: only 3 disagree (Maneco Galeano, born Mexico, is a documented Paraguayan musician; Pellegrini; Watthanachirawat), all hold citizenship of the country, so none is a Tina-Turner-class error. build-extra-artists.mjs now pulls P19/P740 birth/formation country alongside P27 and drops a candidate only when origin is KNOWN, differs, AND they hold no citizenship of the country — keeping exile-born and naturalised nationals. Famous expats cannot reach the gap-fill anyway: they are in MB and the dedup step removes them. Residual risk, stated plainly: birth-vs-career edge cases remain (Joséphine Baker moves CH-style to the US), and 79 flagged artists have no Wikidata entry so they were left alone — the raw 518 sit in data/origin-corrections-work.json for a verified pass later.
- UNIFIED PANEL LIST — GAP-FILL IS INVISIBLE TO GUESTS (Aug 6, 2026, same-day rework of the "From the crates" section): MusicBrainz and Discogs/Wikidata entries now render as ONE indistinguishable list. THE RULE, RESTATED: "never merge" is a BACKEND rule (separate storage in extra-artists.json, separate dedup, MB canonical, gap-fill only) — it must NEVER leak into what visitors see. REMOVED: the FromTheCrates component + CSS, its heading, its explainer, its source attribution in the main flow, and the wide fake-looking year ranges that showed even on a specific year. NOW: one render pool (PanelPoolArtist = PoolArtist + optional externalUrl/undated) feeding the SAME pills, tiers (5→20→+20→100 cap), and genre dropdown. ORDER: MB first (tag-weight ranked), then era-dated gap-fill in press-count order, then undated last with a quiet "· undated" tag — no cross-source ranking is invented because MB tag votes and Discogs pressing counts share no scale. ONLY VISIBLE DIFFERENCE: an MB pill resolves to its Ear Clef page / smart link; a gap-fill pill opens its Discogs or Wikidata page (same pill, same ▶ affordance). COUNT: header shows the merged total for the place+year (PY 1989 = 227 = 47 MB + 36 era-dated + 144 undated) — one honest number beats exposing internal plumbing. GENRE DROPDOWN merges both pools (Discogs styles lowercased so they pool with MB tags): PY 1989 now offers polka·114, guarania·62, chamamé·18 — genres MB alone could never surface. PLAY QUEUE STAYS MB-ONLY (passed mbPool, not pool): the resolver era-picks from MB release-group dates and keys its permanent cache by MBID, which gap-fill entries have by definition — forcing them in would mean name-only YouTube guesses with no era-correctness and no cache key, so it was NOT done. VERIFIED PY 1989: one h3, zero "From the crates" strings, Terceto Ñamandu / Aparicio Gonzalez / Los Joker's / Pro Rock Ensamble sitting at ranks 47–53 mid-list with Discogs destinations, all 17 undated entries below every dated one (first undated index 83 > last dated 82), 100-pill cap intact.
- GAP-FILL SESSION 1 — DISCOGS + WIKIDATA, LAOS/PARAGUAY PILOT (Aug 7, 2026; scoping proposal approved): NEW pipeline scripts/build-extra-artists.mjs adds artists MusicBrainz has NO record of at all — never merges, never reconciles, MB STAYS CANONICAL. Runs LOCALLY and commits JSON, so DISCOGS_TOKEN lives in .env.local and is NOT needed in Netlify (the live site never calls Discogs). PIPELINE: (1) Wikidata SPARQL per country → dated canon + the MB/Discogs ID crosswalk (a P434 = MB already knows them → dedup fuel, never a candidate); (2) Discogs country release sweep (paged, ~60/min authed) → artist names parsed off "Artist – Title" display strings (Discogs search returns NO structured credits), stripping the "(21)" disambiguation suffix, dropping Various/Unknown/multi-artist credits; (3) DEDUP = CONSERVATIVE BY DESIGN, skip on ANY doubt: exact normalized name in MB, alias match, OR score≥90 with either-way containment all skip; (4) survivors get a Discogs artist-id lookup for the link-out. Resumable via data/extra-artists-work.json (gitignored). PILOT RESULTS: LAOS 160 Discogs releases + 8 WD musicians → 108 candidates → 99 NEW (9 skipped: 4 exact/1 alias/4 fuzzy); PARAGUAY 1,370 releases + 119 WD → 708 candidates → 394 NEW (314 skipped: 259 exact/4 alias/38 fuzzy/13 crosswalk). Total 493 artists MB never had. DATA-QUALITY FINDING (drove a design change): Laos is 82/99 UNDATED — Discogs doesn't date those vintage Lao pressings either, exactly as scoping predicted — so extraArtistsFor() returns TWO GROUPS and the panel never blends them: era matches (release span ±5y) under the normal list, undated ones under their own honest heading ("No dated Laos pressings for 1975 — but the crates hold these, undated in the source"). Showing undated acts as if they belonged to the selected year would have been the easy bug. PANEL: components/explore/FromTheCrates.tsx renders "From the crates" BELOW the MB list, visually separated, with the standing line "Beyond MusicBrainz — {country} artists documented by Discogs pressings and Wikidata. Not counted above." and per-artist link-outs (Discogs artist page → Wikidata → Discogs search fallback). It NEVER enters the MB count/list/queue. VERIFIED: PY 1989 (the manually-inspected scoping combo) shows Terceto Ñamandu, Los Joker's, Aparicio Gonzalez, Dúo Méndez-López, Grupo Ñamandu — real guarania/polca, dated — plus 138 undated below; LA 1975 correctly shows zero dated and the all-undated framing. SESSION 2 (not started): roll out to the remaining sparse countries (gate: pre-2000 MB releases < ~500). NOTE: parsed names keep verbose compound credits ("Oscar Pérez Con La Alegre Fórmula Nueva") — real, if wordy; a future pass could split them.
- MOBILE SLIDER CRASH FIXED (Aug 6, 2026 — reproduced on two phones: fast drags / full drag to 2026 killed the page): ROOT CAUSE = the URL-writer ran history.replaceState on EVERY slider tick, and iOS Safari THROWS a SecurityError past ~100 calls/30s — a fast drag is hundreds of ticks, and the quota window explains "several times in a row" (first drag eats the quota, the next throws instantly). Two accomplices amplified the lock-up: per-tick applyHeat (200+ polygon altitude transitions) and per-tick remounts of any open panel (key includes year → fetch storms, QueuePlayer teardown). The 2026 right edge was NOT a separate bug (all boundary math is guarded — zero-max heat → 0, missing year keys → 0); dragging to the edge is simply the longest fastest drag. FIXES: (1) YearSlider throttles commits to ~6/s with a guaranteed commit on release; the thumb/readout track every tick locally. ECHO-GUARD SUBTLETY (bug found in verification): React can deliver an EARLIER commit's prop echo AFTER a later one — "differs from last sent" alone yanks the thumb back; any prop change within 600ms of a send is treated as an echo. (2) URL writer debounced 500ms + replaceState wrapped in try/catch with a beacon — it can NEVER kill the page again. (3) Panels key off a SETTLED panelYear (350ms after the year stops moving; programmatic jumps — surprise, deep links — set it directly so panels open without the wait). (4) NEW GLOBAL BEACONS: uncaught errors + unhandled rejections on the explore page report to /api/postcard (context "uncaught", 5s rate-limited) — future page-deaths self-report. MEASURED (380px, violent 127-tick full drag): replaceState 127→2 calls, panel remounts ~127→~2, settle lands exactly on 2026 with URL/readout/panel in agreement.
- ORIGIN-ONLY PANEL (Aug 6, 2026 — LOCKED EDITORIAL POLICY): the explore panel shows artists FROM a place, never artists whose records merely got distributed there (distribution reach ≠ local culture — the ATB-in-Laos rule). REMOVED from every surface (countries, states, widened views, surprise landings): the "N releases issued here" line + its issued-or-distributed explainer, the "On these releases" credited-artists section (and its tiers, from 63206b1), and the collapsed "Released in {place} · N" releases fold — all were distribution windows wearing artist names. The from-here list IS the panel now, with the honest count in its header: "Top artists from Paraguay · 47" (count = the full pool for that place+era, ≤300 pool cap, NOT the rendered tier; genre filter selected → the genre window's count). Release data still feeds the heat map, totals math, and listen-link discrimination UNDER THE HOOD — it just never renders as people. Sparse places show thinner panels ON PURPOSE (Laos 1989–99 widened: honest empty note, zero foreign credits — verified); empty/sparse notes + nearby-years widen (now keyed off pool size, not release totals) unchanged; What-was-playing stays THE one sanctioned window into non-local music (its framing is culture-not-origin). Play queues confirmed pool-only (QueuePlayer never touched release credits). Server response shape unchanged (old cached clients + under-the-hood uses keep working); dead client code removed (credits tiers, releases fold, releaseSearchHref, isSubdivision copy switch).
- PLACE+ERA PLAY QUEUES (Aug 6, 2026 — discovery ends in sound): every country/state panel gets "▶ Play {place} {year}" (prominent gold pill after the totals line — right where a Surprise landing puts your thumb). ARCHITECTURE (hybrid lazy cache, resolved PER-ARTIST for cross-combo reuse): the queue is a deterministic client walk of the panel's popularity pool (top 40 attempts → typically ~25-30 tracks); each artist resolves via /api/queue/artist/[mbid]/[decade]?name= — (1) MB release-groups era pick (in-decade first, Single>EP>Album, closest to mid-decade; ±15y 'nearby' fallback; else 'catalog'), (2) MB url-rels YouTube channel → uploads title-match (CHEAP: ~2-4 quota units), (3) else ONE search.list capped fallback (100 units) filtered to exact-channel-name or "- Topic" uploads ONLY (fan uploads fail the bar), (4) every candidate passes videos.list public+embeddable before shipping. CACHE: Blobs "queue" store key artist/<mbid>/<decade>, PERMANENT (nulls retry after 30d) + 30d CDN — Marley resolved once serves every 70s Jamaica combo forever; second visitor = all cache hits, instant. No combo-level store and no open write endpoints (the walk is deterministic, so per-artist caching IS the queue cache). QUOTA (measured): search-path artists ≈101 units, channel-path ≈4; a fresh 40-artist queue ≈ 2,000–2,700 units → ~3–4 NEW queues/day on the 10k free quota, each permanent; quotaExceeded → uncached {quota:true} 503 → UI "This one's still brewing — try again in a bit." (partial queues play what they have and finish on a later day). PLAYER: visible YouTube IFrame API embed in-panel (ToS-safe), autoplay on tap, prev/next/auto-advance, numbered queue list with roster links (roster mbid map), jump-to-track. HONESTY: pool <5 → no play button, "too few artists on record" note; build finishing <5 tracks → "Only N verified tracks here — honest, not padded"; era labels in-era/nearby/catalog stored per track. VERIFIED QUEUES: JM 1972 → Marley (official channel), King Tubby, Jimmy Cliff, Toots, Gregory Isaacs, Peter Tosh "You Can't Blame the Youth", Horace Andy "Get Wise" (7/10 resolve, ALL in-era); US-WA 1992 → Nirvana, Alice in Chains, Soundgarden, Heart, Chris Cornell, Bikini Kill, Unwound (7/10, all in-era). Zero Anthropic wallet. NOTE: dev (`next dev`) has no Blobs context → resolves recompute each time (quota!) — verify sparingly locally; prod caches. /refresh-earclef can pre-warm popular combos later by walking pools through the same endpoint.
- RELEASES-CREDITS PARITY + WIDEN-PILL POLISH (Aug 6, 2026 — from Stefano's mobile Laos test): (1) the "On these releases" credits view (shown when a place has no origin-artist pool) now gets the same tier treatment (5 → 20 → +20 → 100 cap, Show fewer, "Top 100 credited artists shown." note) — server upgraded to feed it: RELEASE_LIMIT 30→100 (SAME single MB request), credited artists ranked by how many issued-here releases they're on (was first-12-seen), capped 100; blob key bumped panel/v2/ → panel/v3/. NO GENRE DROPDOWN on this surface, by honesty: MB release responses carry no artist tags, and per-artist tag lookups would be an MB fan-out per panel — tiers only (Laos 1989–99: 83 credited artists from 100 releases, was 12 from 30; Roy Orbison pressed in 90s Laos is real). (2) The widen-mode pills ("Show nearby years", "← Back to N only", both panels) had inert align-self (.body isn't flex) and sat low/off-center on the text baseline — new .widen class (block-level, fit-content width, real margins, same retry-pill look); verified desktop + 375px (pill flush with the panel inset, no overflow).
- "SURPRISE ME" (Aug 6, 2026 — one-tap serendipity): the yin-yang mark as a glass-pill button labeled "Surprise me", PLACED directly under the search box (top-left of the stage; matches the search's styling, never crowds the globe; mark rotates 180° on hover, spins while working). ZERO-WALLET: selection is a client-side weighted mixture over committed data only (lib/explore/surprise.ts) — 30% documented "What was playing" eras (page.tsx passes {country,from,to} triples server-side; stories never ship), 40% substantial country+year (country UNIFORM for variety, year weighted by that country's release counts ≥25), 20% states (state uniform, year weighted by artist emergence), 10% sparse wildcard (3–24 releases — obscure-but-real). Data (country counts, state counts, country names from the geojson) loads lazily on first tap via URLs the globe already fetched (HTTP-cache warm). ON TAP: year TWEENS to the target (12 steps/~540ms — the heat map time-travels), then the existing focusRequest flow flies the camera (states dip below the layer threshold) and opens the panel; no-WebGL fallback skips the flight and just opens (verified via ?noglobe=1). SPOTLIGHT: CountryPanel prop `spotlight` (ExploreClient passes it when the open panel matches the surprise's place:year) renders a gold "Your surprise" card — ONE rank-weighted draw from the pool's top 8 (weight 1/(rank+1.5), stable per mount) with its top tags; What-was-playing shows via existing panel behavior when a combo matches. NO REPEATS per session (seen-set of place:year keys; falls back to a repeat only if ~25 draws all collide). Shareable by construction (?y=&c=). Verified taps: Alaska 1966 → spotlight Stan Jones (state layer swapped in), Guyana 1978 (wildcard), Mongolia 2022 → spotlight The HU (fallback mode, no flight).
- PANEL DISCOVERY: TIERS + "FILTER BY GENRE" DROPDOWN (Aug 6, 2026; same-day rework of the chips+name-search first cut — chips and the name box are GONE, name-seekers belong on /artists or search engines): the explore panel is a digging tool. ARCHITECTURE: responses carry a `panelArtists` discovery pool — era-active artists in popularity order WITH their tags (≤300; states from the committed dataset's spam-filtered tags, countries from the origin sweep's MB tags) — so everything below is client-side, zero extra requests. Blob cache key bumped panel/ → panel/v2/ (v1 entries age out; pool-less cached responses degrade to the old top-12, no filter control). READING ORDER: header → "[N] releases/artists" line → ♪ Filter by genre → TOP [GENRE] ARTISTS FROM [PLACE] → pills → cap note. TIERS: 5 → Show more (20) → Show next 20 (40/60/80/100 hard render cap) → Show fewer; strict popularity order, and the tiers/cap apply WITHIN a selected genre — artists invisible in the overall top 100 become reachable through their genre window (verified: piano blues in Mississippi 1930 surfaces Blind John Davis). FILTER BY GENRE: searchable dropdown listing EVERY pool tag with counts ("piano blues · 2 artists"), prevalence-ordered (ties alphabetical), tiny scenes included BY DESIGN (no threshold — that's the point of the rework); "Type to narrow…" search appears at >12 options; options render-capped at 250 (searchable, so nothing is unreachable); place-name tags excluded ("finland" isn't a genre); selecting closes the list, resets the tier to 5, retitles the header ("Top piano blues artists from Mississippi"); ✕ or "All genres" clears. GLOBAL-LENS COMPAT: ?genre= still filters server-side; the panel opens pre-filtered and the dropdown (lens tag excluded) digs into sub-genres. AT CAP: "Top 100 shown — filter by genre to dig deeper." (genre selected: "Top 100 <genre> artists shown."). Verified on CA 2005: 250 options, "hip" → hip hop·26/west coast hip hop·14, 100 pills exactly at cap. Hard guarantee: never more than 100 artist pills in the DOM.
- SINGLE-YEAR EXPLORE (Aug 6, 2026 — replaces the July 17 dual-thumb range): the slider picks ONE year; the two-thumb range read as "a number that's secretly a range" to first-timers and wide spans were the heaviest queries. URLS: ?y=1969 (written only when ≠ default); old ?from/?to links MIGRATE to their midpoint year (from=1965&to=1975 → y=1970, verified). SPARSE UX ("show nearby years"): when a panel lands thin (<5 results, incl. 0) it offers a one-tap "Show nearby years (Y−5–Y+5) →" that re-fetches ±5 through the STILL-SPAN-CAPABLE API and relabels the header "Y−5–Y+5 · around your year" with a "← Back to Y only" toggle; widen state is panel-local (parent keys panels by country+year, so it resets on any year/place change; slider never moves). Same pattern in ArtistEraPanel's empty state. SERVER UNCHANGED ON PURPOSE: span parsing stays in every route — the widen needs it, and CDN-cached old clients still send spans. What-was-playing needed NOTHING: findPlayingEntry's overlap scoring already matches a single-year pin inside an era (1970 → JM 1960–74, verified live). PERF, measured honestly: raw uncached MB latency is span-independent (same 3-request fan-out; CR/1988 6.7s vs CR/1950–2000 2.0s = MB load noise) — the win is CACHE CONVERGENCE: combo space per country collapses ~8,000 spans → 127 years, so the 30d CDN/Blobs caches go warm quickly and repeat combos answer in ms; state panels serve ~10–12ms from the committed dataset for any span (single-year pure win). Also fixed in passing: panel total copy now pluralizes ("1 artist", was "1 artists"). GlobeScene keeps [year, year] internally so the span-capable heat helpers are untouched.
- ALL 50 STATES ON THE GLOBE (Aug 6, 2026 — the Hawaii treatment, full-roster): every US state + DC is clickable/searchable with its own artist panel. UX DECISION: ZOOM DECIDES — zoomed out the US is one country (whole-USA click/panel byte-for-byte unchanged); crossing altitude 1.15 swaps the US polygon for 51 state features (hysteresis exits at 1.3 so the border never flaps; state clicks and search fly-tos land at 0.95 to keep the layer lit; zoomed-out US tooltip says "zoom in for the fifty states"; no-WebGL fallback lists all states under United States). ARCHITECTURE: states CANNOT ride the Hawaii bbox-carve (the mainland is one polygon) — they come from public/data/us-states-110m.geojson (Natural Earth admin-1, stripped to {ADMIN, ISO_A2:"US-XX"}, 46KB, lazy-loaded on first zoom-in; Alaska stays west of the antimeridian at 110m, no wrap). PANELS SERVE FROM COMMITTED DATA (playing.json pattern): scripts/build-state-data.mjs swept MusicBrainz — discovery `(area:"X" OR beginarea:"X")` over 334 unique names from lib/explore/us-states.json (state + curated music cities per state, Muscle Shoals/Asbury Park/Eau Claire-grade scenes included; beginarea is ESSENTIAL — The Killers carry area=United States, begin-area=Las Vegas), then PRECISE assignment by walking each hit area's "part of" parents to its ISO-3166-2 state (name collisions like Portland/Manchester/Birmingham cost sweep pages, never accuracy). RESULT: 102,511 artists assigned across 51 states → lib/explore/state-artists.json (3MB, top-400-overall ∪ top-30-per-decade per state, ~24k stored) + public/data/state-year-counts.json (emergence histograms → the zoomed-in choropleth; 1969 lights CA/NY white-hot, plains dim). Coverage: CA 11.4k / NY 9.3k / TX 7k … WY 81 (honest sparse; even WY's top are real — Teenage Bottlerocket is from Laramie). NEVADA (personal): 1,093 artists, top = Imagine Dragons, The Killers, Panic! at the Disco, Five Finger Death Punch — Vegas surfaces properly. RANKING: tag-vote weight with spam killed at aggregate — tags derived from the artist's own name and tags carried by <3 artists pool-wide earn nothing (caught self-taggers outranking Bruno Mars). ERA HONESTY: undated MB artists (mostly modern self-registrations) are excluded from spans ending before 1980 (list AND count — no bedroom producers in 1955 Nevada) and rank after era-documented artists otherwise; person begin dates keep the +15 career offset. Genre lens works offline via stored top-6 credible tags (totals in lens mode = stored-roster floor, honest). ROUTE: /api/explore/[country]/[year] answers US-XX from the file in ms (no MB, no Blobs; US-HI now file-served too — 843 artists vs the old live query's 62); states missing from the file fall back to the live Hawaii-style area query. SEARCH: state names + aliases ("DC", "Washington State") intercept before MB, and the parent walk now returns a US state when it finds one — "Las Vegas" opens Nevada, "Nashville" opens Tennessee (non-US cities unchanged: Manchester→GB). Deep links ?c=US-XX work (fly-to waits for the layer, panel opens immediately). Barack Obama appearing in Hawaii 1960–80 is MusicBrainz being faithful (real MB artist, Grammy-winning audiobooks) — same policy as the Antarctica note. Precompute is resumable (data/state-precompute-work.json v2, gitignored; ~2.5h run, area→state cache preserved across format bumps); refresh = re-run script, commit outputs. Coverage report committed at data/state-coverage-report.json. What-was-playing for states: honest muted line (no state combos in playing.json yet — matcher accepts only country codes).
- OLD-SAFARI PANEL BUG SOLVED (Aug 5, 2026, follow-up — the iPhone 12 mystery CRACKED): friend's answers (instant failure, BOTH WiFi+cellular, every country) ruled out network entirely — root cause was OURS: the July 28 deadline() helper used AbortSignal.any (Safari 17.4+ ONLY; .timeout needs 16+), so on older iOS EVERY panel tap threw a TypeError instantly — masked first as the timeout message (pre-Aug-5 conflation), then as "connection dropped". Timeline matched both his screenshots exactly. FIXES: (1) deadline() feature-detects AbortSignal.any/timeout with a manual AbortController+setTimeout fallback — VERIFIED by deleting both APIs in the pane and loading a panel (the friend's environment, emulated, passing); (2) failure beaconing is now fire-and-forget (no awaited dynamic import in the error path — a blocked/failed reporting chunk can never break the error UI); (3) beacon route RENAMED /api/client-log → /api/postcard ("log"-shaped paths are ad-block-list bait and eat the evidence; old path kept as an alias for deployed clients; lib/clientLog posts to the new path). LESSON (standing): feature-detect modern web APIs in CLIENT code — Safari lags Chrome by years and the pane browser is Chromium, so this class of bug is INVISIBLE in our own testing; check caniuse for anything newer than ~2022 before shipping client-side. Historical beacon retrieval: CLI is tail-only; dashboard (Logs → Functions, [client-error]) — his 7:42 PM Pacific beacons should carry detail "TypeError: AbortSignal.any is not a function", confirming. — iPhone 12/weak-LTE field report: globe + error state worked as designed, but EVERY panel query "timed out"): DIAGNOSIS — payloads are tiny (~2.5KB brotli, one round trip; compression verified), the cost is server hold time on UNCACHED combos (5–20s MB fan-out), and the old client error path CONFLATED instant network drops with 30s timeouts (any fetch rejection showed the timeout copy — the friend's "timeouts" may have been fast LTE connection drops). FIXES: (1) panelData resilientFetch — one SILENT auto-retry (1.2s gap) before any error surfaces; genuine-timeout vs connection-drop messages differentiated by elapsed time; final failures BEACON to client-log with real elapsed ms + attempt count ("country CU/1969 failed after 2 attempts (1551ms, network)") so field timing is no longer guesswork. (2) Blobs read-through cache on the [country]/[year] route (store "explore", key panel/<country:span:genre>, 30d TTL, best-effort writes): once ANY instance computes a combo, repeats answer in ms regardless of cold starts/CDN misses — MB fan-out becomes a once-per-combo cost. (3) iOS SMART-LINK FIX (found in audit): CountryPanel artist pills did async-resolve-then-window.open — iOS Safari blocks popups outside the tap's call stack, so artist links silently did NOTHING on iPhones; now a tab is claimed SYNCHRONOUSLY in the gesture and pointed at the resolved href, with same-tab navigation fallback when popups are blocked (pane-verified: fallback navigates; dead clicks impossible). NOTES: iOS-Safari fetch-kill on backgrounding is unverifiable in the pane (Chromium, no UA spoof) — the auto-retry + beacons are the mitigation + instrumentation; netlify CLI IS logged in (Stefano) but function logs are tail-only via CLI — historical beacons need the dashboard (Netlify → Logs → Functions, filter [client-error]). the weekly zero-wallet refresh is now a command doc (.claude/commands/refresh-earclef.md — Phase A blurb warm via list-blurb-misses + in-session authoring + seed-blurbs ≤60/batch; Phase B 1–3 What-was-playing combos under the standard gate + year-roll pass on the 2020–26 cards; Phase C story-card coverage check; close-out = validate/build/one-push/handoff log line). REFRESH LOG — Aug 6: blurbs 318 misses → 258 (newest 60 authored in-session + seeded; the ENTIRE visible feed window is now warm — remainder is deep-window backlog). Combo added: JP 1968–1975 "The Oricon years — enka, novelty, and the folk turn" (CHARTED — Japan had no national chart before Oricon's launch 4 Jan 1968, so the card starts exactly there; 10 items counted week-by-week off the Oricon weekly number-one tables). Research agent CAUGHT ITS OWN TOOLING FABRICATING TABLE ROWS (a summarizing fetch invented a 1973 'Kandagawa/Others' row) and re-fetched raw wikitext via ?action=raw — STANDING LESSON: for chart tables, read raw source, never a summarizer. Excluded on the gate: Misora Hibari (zero Oricon #1s in-window — her signature hits predate the chart; documented touchstone only), Happy End (NO verifiable chart position ever — in the story as influence + the nihongo rock ronsō, never as an item), Group Sounds' decline (no sourced account found). Traps baked in: Kuroneko no Tango is a Japanese version of Italy's "Volevo un gatto nero", Shiretoko Ryojō is a Morishige cover, Oyoge! Taiyaki-kun (Dec 1975) is a 1976 annual #1 and stays OUT. YEAR-ROLL: NOT a no-op this week — two real 2026 milestones added off the number-one tables: GB Sam Fender & Olivia Dean "Rein Me In" (four separate runs Feb–Jul 2026 = NINETEEN non-consecutive weeks, longest in UK chart history; run arithmetic 3+5+5+6 verified off the table itself, officialcharts.com 403s bots as usual so it stays a link-out) and US Ella Langley "Choosin' Texas" (16 weeks, 2026's longest run so far, confirmed in both the lead and the artist-weeks table). No `to:` extensions needed (cards already run through 2026; next roll at the 2027 boundary). Story cards: FULL COVERAGE 102/102. — REFRESH LOG — Jul 31: blurbs 397 misses found (42-artist roster growth), newest 120 authored in-session + seeded (misses → 277; remainder = older/deep-window items, next runs chip away); combo added CU 1950–59 "Mambo, cha-cha-chá, and the cabaret age" (documented; Sublette-anchored; UCLA frontera URL from the July research is now DEAD — dropped, 3 live sources); year-roll no-op (2020–26 cards current; next meaningful roll at the 2027 boundary); story cards FULL COVERAGE 102/102 (the /add-artist pipeline held).
- DEMO-BUG HARDENING: GLOBE FALLBACK + PANEL TIMEOUTS (July 28, 2026 — two field failures from a live demo on a foreign machine): (1) GLOBE PERSISTENT FAILURE: root cause class = WebGL unavailable (hardware accel off / GPU blocklisted / remote desktop — persistent across reloads, matching the report), with geojson fetch-blocking and chunk failure as secondary paths. FIXES: staged init with per-stage classification; countries geojson retries 3× with backoff AND cache-busting query on later attempts (poisoned-cache-proof); explicit WebGL capability probe; `new Globe()` construction wrapped (context creation can throw even when the probe passes); EVERY failure path now renders a FUNCTIONAL fallback — honest note + full alphabetical clickable country list (panel opens per country; search still works; deep links still open via onDataSourceChange('live') announced from fallback paths). `?noglobe=1` forces the fallback (testing + weak devices) — NOTE: the param must be captured at ExploreClient FIRST RENDER and threaded as a prop (forceFallback) because the URL-writer effect wipes unknown params before the globe's async init could read them (that ordering bug ate the first implementation). (2) CLIENT ERROR REPORTING: lib/clientLog.ts reportClientError → POST /api/client-log (no auth/storage; size-capped; console.error server-side so field failures land in Netlify function logs as [client-error] context|reason|detail|ua). (3) GENRE+COUNTRY HANG (electronic+US stuck loading forever): prod API measured healthy (3-7s all spans) — root cause = NO CLIENT TIMEOUT + no retry UI, so any wedged request (MB slowness pushing the function past its budget → dead connection) spun the skeleton forever. FIXES: MB fetches carry AbortSignal.timeout(8s) in both explore routes; genre-mode origin sweep reduced to ONE page of 100 (electronic-sized tag queries; top-12 unaffected); client panel/search fetches wrapped in AbortSignal.any(caller, 30s timeout) with honest timeout copy; CountryPanel + ArtistEraPanel error states gained a Try-again button (attempt counter re-runs the effect; verified end-to-end with a fetch-failure shim — dev StrictMode double-mount ate single-shot failure simulations, arm twice when testing this way). all 102 artists researched by 6 parallel agents under the proof-batch gate (IMDb suggestion API for id resolution since imdb.com blocks fetchers, facts corroborated on fetched-200 Wikipedia/publisher/press pages, canonical-but-bot-blocked URLs allowed as link-outs when facts verify elsewhere). RESULT: 56 of 102 have sections (6 proof + 50 rollout), 46 correctly have NONE. Standout additions: miles-davis (Malle score, American Book Award autobiography, documented painting career, Dingo role), herbie-hancock (OSCAR for Round Midnight + Blow-Up + Viking memoir), the-beatles (A Hard Day's Night/Help!/Yellow Submarine + Lennon's In His Own Write), michael-jackson (The Wiz, Moonwalk, Captain EO), tenacious-d (Jack Black's career + Pick of Destiny), gorillaz (Hewlett's Tank Girl + Saatchi show + Monkey opera), led-zeppelin (Page + JPJ solo scores), pink-floyd (More + La Vallée), queen (Flash Gordon), tori-amos (2 books + National Theatre musical), imogen-heap (Cursed Child), st-vincent (Nowhere Inn + XX), erykah-badu, joanna-newsom (Inherent Vice), oumou-sangare (Hawa), mdou-moctar (Akounak), the-boswell-sisters (Connee in Kiss the Boys Goodbye 1941). CONSISTENCY RULE APPLIED: member work lives on the member's OWN page when they have one — radiohead + the-smile stay NONE (Yorke/Greenwood covered on their pages), RHCP carries only Kiedis' Scar Tissue (Flea's on his). GATE CATCHES (all excluded): Aurora's Frozen II is soundtrack-vocal only; Bon Iver's known-fors are song placements not scores; a Tame Impala impostor IMDb id carries others' score credits; MF DOOM's IMDb actor known-fors are posthumous syncs; Suuns' claimed score credit conflicts with the film's credited composer; santigold/ratatat/chichi-peralta/richard-cheese claims unverifiable; nick-drake WARNING — the poet/screenwriter Nick Drake (b. 1961, One Life co-writer) is a DIFFERENT LIVING PERSON, never conflate. Low Roar's Death Stranding included as the iconic-sync case (Kojima built the game's musical identity on the band).
- BEYOND THE MUSIC SECTION (July 26, 2026 — PROOF BATCH, full-roster rollout awaiting Stefano's go — SUPERSEDED by the full rollout above): new optional content section `beyond` {enabled, items:[{kind: film-role|film-score|screen-sync|book|art|other, title, context (one factual line), url (https link-out, merch model), linkLabel?}]} — components/Beyond.tsx (section 07, after Press, conditional "Beyond" nav anchor via SiteNav includeBeyond), validator enforces kinds/context/https, /add-artist doc gains the research step (IMDb = tier-1 authority for roles; publishers/retailers for books; OMIT the section rather than pad — most artists have nothing notable). PROOF BATCH (all IMDb pages browser-verified — IMDb 403s plain fetchers; other URLs fetched 200): flea (4 film roles incl. (2) SUBSTANCE FILTER (lib/feed/substance.ts, deterministic, zero AI): a video EARNS the slot if its title names a work (quotes / Official Video / Live at / song-at-Venue with capitalized venue / feat. / Artist–Title dash) OR its RSS media:description keeps ≥80 chars of prose after stripping URLs/hashtags/@handles/emoji/promo phrases (phrases ONLY — eating to end-of-clause destroyed legit prose sharing a line with a CTA); classified at snapshot build from the RSS description YouTube already ships (no extra fetches); failures mark substance:'filler'. Back-compat: fallback/live items and old snapshots carry no substance field → treated substantive. TAKES EFFECT at the next snapshot rebuild (00:20 UTC cron, or immediately when the roster next grows); the client demotion/weave changes are live at deploy. Afrobeat note: Nigeria's 2010s dataset peak partly reflects afrobeats tag-bleed — the story addresses it honestly. — TIERED AUTHORITY MODEL, NO REVIEW QUEUE (July 20, 2026): permanent replacement for the flat "2 sources" rule, applied to the farm engine, the rescore path, and /add-artist. TIER 1 (ONE source publishes a claim when that source is THE authority for that specific fact): awarding body for awards (grammy.com…); Billboard/Official Charts Company for chart positions/records; IMDb or the production's official source for film/TV; MusicBrainz structured record for discography/membership/release dates; the artist's own site or own published words for their own history. GUARDRAILS: authority must match the fact's domain (grammy.com ≠ birthplaces); an artist's own properties NEVER verify self-promotional superlatives ("best-selling ever", "first to…" need an independent reputable source); Wikipedia is reputable, never authoritative. TIER 2 (2+ reputable sources): interpretive/anecdotal claims where no single authority exists. TRIM DON'T REJECT: unverified incidental clauses get cut (never hedged); a failed CORE gets a salvage rewrite around the verified claims (new salvageCard call, may return discard); a card is DISCARDED only when nothing verifiable survives. NO DRAFTS EVER: the farm emits status published|rejected only — the live feed never waits on owner review; /studio's drafts panel is a legacy/audit surface that stays empty by design. Implementation: verifier now returns authoritativeSourceIndexes per claim (tier definitions live in the verify prompt); gate = tier1(authoritative supporter) || tier2(2+ supporters incl. the MB structured record); work files keep rejected cards + claimReport (with tier) for audit; assemble excludes rejected. The existing 155 published cards are untouched. all AI/browser work for roster growth now happens in Claude Code on the owner's plan; the live site only SERVES committed/cached data. NEW /add-artist COMMAND (.claude/commands/add-artist.md): the full pipeline — verified research (agents+browser), content JSON, in-session story-card generation under the farm's gate rules, validation, single push, post-deploy blurb warm. SERVER DE-API AUDIT + SWITCHES: (1) story cards were ALREADY serve-only (/api/stories* reads cards.json + Blobs decisions; generation always local scripts) — confirmed clean; (2) GENRE STORIES: the 10 generated stories are now COMMITTED to lib/explore/genre-stories.json and the route serves the file only — Anthropic SDK + Blobs generation code removed (new genres: generate locally, commit); (3) FEED BLURBS: /api/feed/blurbs is serve-from-cache ONLY (generation code deleted from lib/feed/blurbs.ts); the single write path is owner-gated POST /api/studio/seed-blurbs (key format v2/<slug>/<type>/<title>, ≤60/batch), fed by scripts/warm-blurbs.mjs — a LOCAL warmer that diffs the prod feed snapshot against the cache (--dry lists misses, --slug scopes, default warms the newest-80 visible window, --all overrides) and generates via the claude CLI on the owner's subscription; (4) DISCOVER: the page-load lazy trigger is REMOVED — the 00:10 UTC scheduled function is now the ONLY generator (accepted ~$1/mo wallet cost; a late pool just serves yesterday's until the cron fires). FARM CLI MODE: build-story-cards.mjs auto-detects the claude CLI and routes model calls through it (subscription) with API-wallet fallback (FORCE_API=1 to force; logs which path). CLI AUTH CAVEAT: nested `claude -p` inside a CC session currently 401s (harness ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL + no persistent CLI login on this machine) — Stefano should run `claude /login` once in a plain terminal to activate the subscription path for scripts; until then, in-session generation (the /add-artist flow, where the session model writes the cards/blurbs itself) is the working zero-wallet path and was used to seed 27 missing feed blurbs. MusicBrainz is a first-check convenience, NOT the ceiling. SERVICE-AGNOSTIC RESOLVER FLOW (works for Spotify/Apple later): (1) check MB url-rels (free/fast); (2) if absent, find candidates ON the platform (web-search agents surface /artists/<ASIN> URLs — never invent ASINs); (3) render-verify each candidate in a real browser (JS-shell sites: tab title "X on Amazon Music Unlimited" after a forced frame + release-list check for name collisions); (4) store verified links permanently in content listen.platforms (the service-agnostic store; presenceFromContent picks them up). RESULT: 27 more verified Amazon Music artist pages added (browser-verified every one) — 38 of 40 artists now have direct links, including aplete (B08B1VQQWM — FIOH Vol. 1 confirmed) and hundred-waters (B007Q5GJ70, resolved by clicking through from a known album page when search found no artist URL). GOING-FORWARD FARM RULE (now rule 0 in the generation prompt): pull only clearly-true, easily-verifiable facts (liner-notes/Wikipedia/AllMusic/official-source grade); prioritize hard facts (membership, discography, awards, film/TV placements, documented collaborations, chart records) over interpretive stories/anecdotes/single-interview quotes; when a fact is solid but a detail is shaky, keep the fact and OMIT the detail — never hold the card, never hedge.
- AMAZON MUSIC ARTIST PAGES + OWNER FAN-SERVICE SETTER (July 19, 2026): Amazon Music was already a picker option with search-URL routing; this adds VERIFIED artist-page links. 11 roster artists gained {platform:"amazonMusic"} listen.platforms entries (aurora, florence, hiatus-kaiyote, michael-jackson, pink-floyd, queen, radiohead, stevie-wonder, tame-impala, the-beatles, tool) — URLs from MusicBrainz url-rels, locale ASINs normalized to music.amazon.com, EACH verified in the rendered browser (music.amazon.com is a JS SPA: plain fetch returns an 11KB shell with no artist name, so server-side verification is impossible — the browser-pane tab title "X on Amazon Music Unlimited" after a forced render frame is the check). The other 29 artists have NO Amazon rel on MB → no page link (honesty rule: search URLs still work for them; nothing invented). Code: validator+platformLabel accept amazonMusic; presenceFromContent carries amazonMusicUrl; absence-reroute chains appleMusic → amazonMusic → YouTube (ServiceAbsenceNote + resolveListenHref). Explore pill chain already preferred links.amazonMusic (artist-links route extracts it from MB) — unchanged. NEW /api/studio/fan-service (owner-gated): set a fan's listenService by email — used to set Stefano's accounts (oneskyally@gmail.com = the only address magic links can currently deliver to, + fiohmemorial@gmail.com) to amazonMusic; fan record wins over localStorage on next signed-in visit. ▶ play buttons + video links stay YouTube by design.
- STORY GATE v2: TWO-TIER VERIFICATION + BATCH REVIEW (July 19, 2026): the v1 gate over-held encyclopedic facts (required 2 live prose pages to restate database facts) and held whole cards over incidental details. Fixed in scripts/build-story-cards.mjs: (1) claims now tagged kind hard|soft + core boolean at generation; HARD facts (birthplace, membership, discography, release years, screen roles, awards, feature credits) verify via ONE confirmed Wikipedia source + the artist's MusicBrainz record agreeing (mbStructured(): artist?inc=release-groups+url-rels+artist-rels+aliases, joined into the verify call as a STRUCTURED SOURCE; cards verified this way carry a MusicBrainz source entry); SOFT claims (interpretive/anecdotal) keep the strict 2-prose-source bar — only those reach the review queue. (2) Incidental failures no longer hold a card: reviseCard() drops/softens the unverified side detail (no "reportedly" hedging allowed — drop or restate) and the card publishes on its verified core; hold ONLY when a core claim is unverified. (3) --rescore mode re-judged all 114 existing drafts (resumable via rescoredAt stamps; per-artist Opus call classifying kind/core + applying the policy): 65 auto-promoted → 106/155 published, 49 held — the held set is now genuinely soft/contested material (anecdotes, chart-longevity claims with no prose confirms, single-interview stories). Ops notes: adaptive thinking can burn the whole max_tokens on hard batches → claude() now auto-retries at double budget on stop_reason max_tokens (up to 64k); structured-output schema rejects minItems>1/maxItems (enforce counts in prompt). (4) /studio batch review: StoriesPanel gained a "Batch by artist" mode (checkboxes, per-artist select-all, sticky Approve/Reject-selected bar; one-by-one editor still there) backed by bulk {ids[],action} on /api/studio/stories + writeDecisions() single read-modify-write.
- STORY CARDS ENGINE + FEED FIXES (July 18-19, 2026, overnight farm): per-artist editorial "story cards" — TRUE clickbait hook + short sourced story + media links. FARM: scripts/build-story-cards.mjs (Opus 4.8 adaptive thinking, 2 calls/artist): generate 2-4 cards decomposed into ATOMIC claims each with 2-3 proposed sources → fetch every source live (Wikipedia via action=parse rendered-HTML strip — the extracts API drops TABLES, which is where award/chart facts live; slightly-wrong wiki titles rescued via search API; other outlets fetched w/ browser UA) → strict verifier call with PER-CLAIM keyword-scored excerpts (claimExcerpt; a shared window can't cover 10 claims against a 60k-char article) → GATE: every claim confirmed by 2+ live sources = published, else draft + holdReason. Structured-output API quirk: array minItems only 0/1, maxItems unsupported — enforce counts in prompt. Gate proven: correctly held "Fred sold out MSG" (Wikipedia: it was a sold-out SKRILLEX show he played at). Model-proposed video IDs NEVER trusted — media = artist's own pre-verified video IDs, YouTube search URLs, or live-checked external links. RESULTS (all 40 artists): 155 cards, 41 published / 114 drafts; work files w/ claimReport detail in data/story-cards-work/ (gitignored), farm log data/story-farm.log; 15 transient fetch-failures mid-run all recovered by resumable retry. STORAGE: lib/stories/cards.json (committed = cached forever) + Blobs "stories" overlay for owner decisions — approving a draft publishes WITHOUT a deploy (no GitHub token needed). SURFACES: /api/stories (+/[slug], 1h CDN), "Story & Press" collapsible section at the bottom of every artist page (client-fetched so approvals surface live), story cards woven into /feed after every 5th item (daily-seeded order), /studio "Story drafts" review panel (pager, hold reason, inline hook/story edit, approve/reject/undo → /api/studio/stories owner-gated). FEED FIXES same push: (1) future-dated releases (e.g. Bonobo Sept 11) no longer pin as permanent #1 — distinct "Coming <date>" dashed strip above the feed, auto-flips into the normal feed on release day; (2) PRECOMPUTED FEED: lib/feed/snapshot.ts (RELATIVE imports — bundled into functions) builds the whole-roster feed (MB backbone + iTunes overlay + YouTube RSS, ~5 min sequential) into Blobs "feed" store via netlify/functions/feed-snapshot-background.mts (daily 00:20 UTC schedule + lazy trigger from /api/feed/snapshot on stale) — a /feed visit is now ONE cached fetch instead of ~100; client falls back to live fan-out only while the first snapshot builds (presence re-attached client-side from the roster prop — not stored in the snapshot); (3) daily-seeded shuffle WITHIN month buckets (mulberry32 on date hash) — recency ordering holds, order rotates daily. build-roster.mjs snapshot now also carries channelId + itunesId for the function. populated past shows (setlist.fm pages individually fetched + confirmed), official-store merch, and reputable press for the July 18 roster additions — verified-only, no padding. Notable verified shows: Queen Live Aid 1985 + final Freddie concert (Knebworth 1986); Pink Floyd Live 8 reunion + last-ever full concert (Earls Court 1994-10-29); Marley's final show (Stanley Theatre 1980 — setlist.fm lists all Marley shows under the "bob-marley-and-the-wailers" URLs); MJ's Diana Wembley night 1988 + final concert (Durban 1997); Fred again.. MSG 2023 (under setlist.fm's "Fred again.., Skrillex & Four Tet" trio entry). HONEST OMISSIONS: Ratatat merch HIDDEN (ratatatmusic.com is now a parking page; the Kung Fu store's officialness verifies only via a 2016 Wayback snapshot — below the live-official-link bar); Stevie Wonder merch HIDDEN (steviewonder.net is a newsletter landing page with zero store link); Burial shows untouched (doesn't perform live) and his merch is the Hyperdub label store (his only official outlet, framed as such); Pompeii 1971 rejected (setlist.fm URL 404s — filmed, audience-less shows aren't listed). Merch stores verified via links in the artists' own homepage HTML (queenonline.com/pinkfloyd.com 403 curl-bot-blocked → fetched with browser UA; michaeljackson.com 403 → store accepted as subdomain of the estate domain; aphextwin.warp.net IS the official store). Press skews Rolling Stone/Billboard/NPR/Guardian/Pitchfork (some outlets block crawlers; every URL fetched HTTP 200).

- STAGE 2 SUBSCRIPTION SKELETON SHIPPED (July 14, 2026, second session — approved same day): the federated membership pattern, prototyped on Aplete. CORE DECISION: a membership year is a ONE-TIME Stripe Checkout payment (mode:'payment'), never a Stripe Billing subscription — auto-renewal is structurally impossible (No-Inertia Principle); renewing stacks from current expiry (never punishes early renewal), so the "Add another year" button always shows. FEDERATION: content JSON `membership` section {enabled, priceUsd, perkTitle, teaser, stripeAccountId?} — stripeAccountId absent = platform account (Aplete now); present = Connect Standard direct charge with application-fee hook (rate constant currently 0 in lib/membership/stripeClient.ts). STORAGE: Netlify Blobs stores "members" (record per artist+email: expiresAt/source stripe|comp/remindedAt/followupAt/renewals) and "universe" (posts JSON + gated media blobs ≤4MB; bigger files via future R2). IDENTITY: passwordless magic links (Resend via plain fetch) → HMAC-signed session cookie (lib/membership/tokens.ts + session.ts, AUTH_SECRET, httpOnly 180d); buying auto-signs-in via the claim route. ROUTES: /api/universe/[slug] (teasers public, full feed for active members, no-store), /api/universe/media/... (cookie-gated, Range-request support for Safari), /api/auth/request-link|verify|signout, /api/membership/checkout|claim|renew, /api/stripe/webhook (raw-body signature verify; claim + webhook idempotent via session id), /api/studio/universe (owner: posts CRUD, comp grant/revoke). UI: UniverseSection (components/universe/, section "∞", last on page, nav anchor = perkTitle) — locked view: teaser cards (title/kind/date only — bodies and media never leave the server) + honest join card + magic-link form; member view: status line, renew, sign-out, feed rendering text/image/audio posts (mini gated audio player; <img> not next/image because the optimizer has no session cookie). /studio gains The Universe panel (publish text/image/audio ≤4MB with client-side duration probe, remove posts, gift/revoke years). Reminder emails: netlify/functions/membership-reminders.mts daily 14:00 UTC — ONE reminder ≤14d before expiry with one-click renew link (signed 30d token → prefilled checkout), ONE gentle follow-up 3–17d after expiry (window capped so a dormant function can't spam old records); both stamped on the record, no-ops without keys. Dev fallbacks everywhere (in-process stores, inline dev sign-in link, 501s with honest UI copy when keys absent). scripts/seed-universe.mjs seeds 3 labeled placeholder posts (idempotent; run against prod after OWNER_KEY lands, then replace content via /studio). Verified end-to-end July 14 (comp grant → magic link → gated feed; media 401 anonymous / 200+206 member; checkout+webhook 501 until keys). Deps: + stripe SDK (Stefano-approved). NOT yet testable: real Stripe test-card flow + webhook + actual emails — blocked on action-queue keys 7–9.

- STRIPE TEST PAYMENT VERIFIED END-TO-END (July 15, 2026): Stefano supplied the sk_test key (local .env.local only; he adds Netlify env himself). Full REAL loop executed against the One Sky Ally LLC sandbox: /api/membership/checkout → Stripe-hosted page (product copy renders: "Aplete — The Universe: one year / $20.00 / no auto-renewal…") → test card 4242… paid ($20.00 USD confirmed via Stripe API) → claim redirect ?joined=1 → member record {source: stripe, expires +1yr, renewals 0, claimedAt set} → cookie set → gated feed open. H1 replay fix verified against the real paid session: replaying the success URL returns 302 → ?signin=needed with ZERO Set-Cookie headers and does NOT extend the membership. - STAGE 2 SECURITY REVIEW + HARDENING (July 14, 2026, second session, same day): security-reviewer agent audit of all membership code — verdict: crypto core clean (HMAC tokens, timingSafeEqual, raw-body webhook verify, no open redirects, no IDOR, React-escaped content, no secrets committed); 2 HIGH + 4 MEDIUM found and the practical ones FIXED same session: (H1) Stripe success URLs no longer mint sign-in cookies on replay — claimedAt on the member record, one cookie per session id, replays redirect to ?signin=needed (banner points to magic-link sign-in); (H2) year-stacking race killed — claim + webhook share a compare-and-set loop via Blobs conditional writes (getMemberSnapshot/putMemberGuarded etags, onlyIfNew|onlyIfMatch; webhook 500s on exhausted contention so Stripe retries); (M1) media responses send X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff + Content-Disposition: attachment on SVG (script-in-SVG can't execute via direct navigation; <img>/<audio> unaffected); (M2) magic-link send throttles moved to shared "auth" Blobs store, keyed per-email (60s) AND per-client-IP (15s) — survives cold starts; (M3) studio uploads reject >6.5MB via Content-Length before buffering; (M4) magic links now SINGLE-USE (atomic claimOnce marker on token signature; replay → signin=expired); (L1) EMAIL_PATTERN strict charset (emails are Blob key segments). Verified live: first link use signs in, replay rejected, throttle engages, feed intact. Accepted-for-prototype leftovers: renew tokens multi-use within 30d (they only prefill checkout, mint nothing), signout has no CSRF token (nuisance only), Range slices from full in-memory buffer (perf), session TTL 180d.

- MUSIC-ONLY FEED v1 SHIPPED (July 12, 2026): /feed "Latest" — reverse-chron releases (MB release-groups w/ dates) + videos (YouTube) across the roster, per-artist video cap 8, badges/covers/dates, clicks → YouTube; nav gains Latest. The 2023 vision's music-only feed, realized as v1. Freshness note: MB can lag day-one drops; iTunes Search API (keyless) identified as the v1.1 freshness overlay if needed.
- DE-SPOTIFY MILESTONE SHIPPED (July 12, 2026): album embeds → AlbumCard (Cover Art Archive artwork + MusicBrainz tracklists, clicks → quoted YouTube searches); "View full catalog" → CatalogPopup (MB release-groups via /api/artist/catalog, categorized Albums/EPs/Singles/Live/Compilations/Remixes/Other); "View all videos" → VideosPopup (/api/artist/videos: YouTube Data API when YOUTUBE_API_KEY is set, else RSS latest-15 fallback with honest note — key still pending from Stefano); Spotify pills/socials removed from all content JSONs (integrations.spotify kept dormant). MB remasters live inside their album release-group (no separate category by design).

### Env keys (current)
- In use — local .env.local AND Netlify: `YOUTUBE_API_KEY`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`.
- In use locally (verified with a live test purchase July 15), Stefano adding to Netlify himself: `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` (sk_test).
- In use locally, PENDING on Netlify: `OWNER_KEY` (studio/follow/universe auth), `AUTH_SECRET` (magic-link + session signing; generated July 14).
- In use locally (live send verified July 15), Stefano adding to Netlify himself: `RESEND_API_KEY`.
- Pending everywhere: `GITHUB_CONTENT_TOKEN` (tier-board retier), `NEXT_PUBLIC_AUDIO_BASE` (R2 cutover; defaults to /audio), `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `EMAIL_FROM` (needed once a Resend domain is verified).
- Never needed after all: SETLISTFM key (URLs only), SPOTIFY creds (de-Spotified; iTunes overlay is keyless).
- All secrets in `.env.local`, never committed. Every page must render from JSON alone when keys are absent (Discover/studio degrade gracefully).

### Known issues
- ~~**OPEN: the panel's CREDITS list serves artists who never claimed the place.**~~ **CLOSED Aug 30, 2026** by the country precompute, which serves origin artists only — there is no releases-derived list left to be wrong. Original report: Clicking Antarctica returns 67 artists including Alanis Morissette, Moby and Major Lazer; the French Southern Lands return Childish Gambino and Kiana Ledé. Cause: that list is built from RELEASES carrying the country code, and digital distributions enumerate every territory on earth — the same poisoned layer that makes `country-year-counts.json` claim 7,618 releases for Antarctica. Standing lesson 4 again: query layers lie, per-record fields are the truth. This is NOT the claim-wins case and must not be confused with it — the 18 artists who FILED themselves in Antarctica appear correctly in the origin list and stand under the owner's ruling; these 67 made no claim about the place at all. Distinct from the fallback-list question, which was ruled Aug 29 and correctly shipped no code.
- ~~`meta.ogImage` points to non-existent files across all artists~~ — RESOLVED Aug 29, 2026, see the OG share cards entry in the build log. Cards are generated per artist at build time; the field is now an opt-in override, honoured only when it names something that exists.
- No test suite (adding one requires a dependency decision; validate-content.mjs + build + lint are the current gate).
- Trust in the Sun Play section serves labeled placeholder tones until the R2 cutover.
- Discover UI can render stale API responses for up to the CDN stale-while-revalidate window after a deploy changes response shape (self-heals).

## 8. Constraints & operating principles

- FIOH owns 2026 (trademark filing July 31; Día de los Muertos activation Nov 1–2). Ear Clef is a low-energy-week project until further notice.
- Stefano is balancing Tao/Visual Artform work shifts, marriage, family, and health recovery — the system exists to protect that balance, not strain it.
- Ship over perfect. Release ideas even when profit is uncertain. Artist-first ethics are non-negotiable in any monetization decision.
- Values filter for every decision: art, creativity, community — creativity as a positive effect on the world.
